# The Environmental Protection (Prescribed Processes and Substances) Regulations 1991

> Text as at 2025-11-01.
>
> Extent: England, Wales, Scotland.

*Extent: E+W+S*

1991 No. 472

ENVIRONMENTAL PROTECTION

The Environmental Protection (Prescribed Processes and Substances) Regulations 1991 *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

Made *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

6th March 1991 *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

Laid before Parliament *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

8th March 1991 *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

Coming into force *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

in England and Wales *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

1st April 1991 *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

in Scotland *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

1st April 1992 *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

The Secretary of State for the Environment as respects England, the Secretary of State for Wales as respects Wales and the Secretary of State for Scotland as respects Scotland in excercise of the powers under section 2 of the Environmental Protection Act 1990 *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29][^f00001] and all other powers enabling them in that behalf make the following Regulations:— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

## 1 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note). Citation, application and commencement (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-79123d4ca51406d5040fa14082242b5e][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

(1 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] These Regulations may be cited as the Environmental Protection (Prescribed Processes and Substances) Regulations 1991. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

(2 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] These Regulations shall come into force in England and Wales on 1st April 1991 and in Scotland on 1st April 1992. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

## 2 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note). Interpretation (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-b6466c6594fa99a491951cdbe2f4d99c][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

In these Regulations— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- “ *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]the Act *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]” means the Environmental Protection Act 1990; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- “background concentration” has the meaning given to that term in regulation 4(7); *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- “Part A process” means a process falling within a description set out in Schedule 1 hereto under the heading ‘Part A’ and “Part B process” means a process falling within a description so set out under the heading ‘Part B’; and *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- “particular matter” means grit, dust or fumes. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

## 3 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note). Prescribed Provisions (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-4c8db641bd04f4d428dcf266779000df][^c-8fcda5c562902b3c722ee8da5125246a][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

(1 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Subject to the following provisions of these Regulations, the descriptions of processes set out in Schedule 1 hereto are hereby prescribed pursuant to section 2(1) of *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] the Act *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] as processes for the carrying on of which after the prescribed date an authorisation is required under section 6. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

(2 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Schedule 2 has effect for the interpretation of Schedule 1. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

(3 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] In paragraph (1), the prescribed date means the appropriate date set out or determined in accordance with Schedule 3 *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] except that, as respects the application of paragraph (bb) of Section 5.1, Part A, in Schedule 1, it means, subject to regulation 3 of the Environmental Protection (Prescribed Processes and Substances) (Amendment) (Hazardous Waste Incineration) Regulations 1998, 13th April 1998[^c-d7ba08eff1be333b6068cdff7b2ccf3f] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

## 3A. Exclusion of prescribed processes under control of the Pollution Prevention and Control (England and Wales) Regulations 2000 (affected by repeal, see note)[^M_E_aha0jehd-p06j-ynfy-y69e-69c428b16bd04][^c-ab5548ccaf1136a765032b69986d3730][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

*Extent: E+W*

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

## 3A. Exclusion of prescribed processes under control of the Environmental Authorisations (Scotland) Regulations 2018[^M_E_07jjfr5p-gtpa-qi7j-mby6-69c428b16bcae][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29][^c-68f648b1bdebbf6951132781c689a785][^c-15c9479bf87abb821b756bdf8453869a]

*Extent: S*

(1) Where a process which is being carried on under an authorisation requires a permit or registration under the Environmental Authorisations (Scotland) Regulations 2018[^c-b894ea47a192cabc902410e8f8ccf39e], that process will no longer be taken to fall within a description in Schedule 1 from the date the permit is granted.

(2) Where a process which is not being carried on under an authorisation requires a permit or registration[^c-75fcca841aeb95d59c483a3b78612f31] under those Regulations, that process will not be taken to fall within a description in Schedule 1 from the date on which the permit or registration is required.

## 4 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note). Exceptions (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-21ffe5a6074a495bf598a397b25ad399][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

(1 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Subject to paragraph (6), a process shall not be taken to be a Part A process if it has the following characteristics, namely— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- (i (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] that it cannot result in the release into the air of any substance prescribed by regulation 6(1) or there is no likelihood that it will result in the release into the air of any such substance except in a quantity which is so trivial that it is incapable of causing harm or its capacity to cause harm is insignificant; and *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (ii (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-2b19905ee636809dd9bc187f13be980e][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] that it cannot result in the release into water of any substance prescribed by regulation 6(2) except— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (a (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] in a concentration which is no greater than the background concentration; or *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (b (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] in a quantity which does not, in any 12 month period, exceed the background quantity by more than the amount specified in relation to the description of substance in column 2 of Schedule 5; and *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (iii (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] that it cannot result in the release into land of any substance prescribed by regulation 6(3) or there is no likelihood that it will result in the release into land of any such substance except in a quantity which is so trivial that it is incapable of causing harm or its capacity to cause harm is insignificant. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

(2 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Subject to paragraph (6), a process shall not be taken to be a Part B process unless it will, or there is a likelihood that it will, result in the release into the air of one or more substances prescribed by regulation 6(1) in a quantity greater than that mentioned in paragraph (1)(i) above. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

(3 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] A process shall not be taken to fall within a description in Schedule 1 if it is carried on in a working museum to demonstrate an industrial process of historic interest or if it is carried on for educational purposes in a school as defined in section 114 of the Education Act 1944 *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29][^f00002] or, in Scotland, section 135(1) of the Education (Scotland) Act 1980 *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29][^f00003]. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

(4 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] The running on or within an aircraft, hovercraft, mechanically propelled road vehicle, railway locomotive or ship or other vessel of an engine which propels *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] or provides electricity for[^c-9e6fd7bee4b5297e40bc99d35986b8f3] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] it shall not be taken to fall within a description in Schedule 1. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

(4A (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-24d1228e250a0c93e6d984936f0b9cb1][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] The running of an engine *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] [^c-5e0f29f2e71c6d64adaa2376200d3253]... in order to test it before installation or in the course of its development shall not be taken to fall within a description in Schedule 1. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

(4B (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-3ee5eae1d461620922e61de5dcbafb08][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] The use of a fume cupboard shall not be taken to fall within a description in Schedule 1 if it is used as a fume cupboard in a laboratory for research or testing, and it is not— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- (a (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] a fume cupboard which is an industrial and continuous production process enclosure; or *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (b (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] a fume cupboard in which substances or materials are manufactured. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

In this paragraph, “fume cupboard” has the meaning given by the British Standard ‘Laboratory fume cupboards’ published by the British Standards Institution numbered BS7258: Part 1: 1990. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

(5 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] A process shall not be taken to fall within a description in Schedule 1 if it is carried on as a domestic activity in connection with a private dwelling. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

(5A (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-e441916081706a507891636ed174dfea][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

(6 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Paragraphs (1) and (2) do not exempt any process described in Schedule 1 from the requirement for authorisation if the process may give rise to an offensive smell noticeable outside the premises where the process is carried on. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

(7 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-a575583a091470b63ce90458052bddac][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] In these Regulations— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- “background concentration” means any concentration of the relevant substance which would be present in the release irrespective of any effect the process may have had on the composition of the release and, without prejudice to the generality of the foregoing, includes such concentration of the substance as is referred to in paragraph (8) below; and *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- “background quantity” means such quantity of the relevant substance as is referred to in paragraph (8) below. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

(8 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] The concentration or, as the case may be, quantity mentioned in paragraph (7) above is such concentration or quantity as is present in— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- (a (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] water supplied to the premises where the process is carried on; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (b (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] water abstracted for use in the process; and *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (c (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] precipitation onto the premises on which the process is carried on. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

## 5. Enforcement (affected by repeal, see note)[^M_E_q67g2xj6-h2mm-0tbm-zd7f-69c42a662a17c][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

*Extent: E+W*

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

## 5. Enforcement[^M_E_xwwcglam-rq2a-7k7j-8w60-69c42a662a0f6][^V_S_20b9e25e-bafb-8c00-2088-ed42daf2a256][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

*Extent: S*

(1) The descriptions of processes set out in Schedule 1 under the heading ‘Part A’ are designated pursuant to section 2(4) of the Act for central control.

(2) Subject to paragraph (3), the descriptions of processes[^c-c29a6430662cbd6ee4829ffb5a07693b] set out in Schedule 1 under the heading ‘Part B’ are so designated for local control.

(3)[^c-b3a0eb9e8211deb35520df833a098175] Where in an SED installation activities are carried out falling within any description of processes set out in Schedule 1 under the heading “Part B”, such processes in such an installation are so designated for central control.

(4) For the purposes of this regulation and regulation 6, “SED installation” shall have the same meaning as it has for the purposes of the Pollution Prevention and Control (Scotland) Regulations 2000.

## 6. Prescribed substances: release into the air, water or land (affected by repeal, see note)[^M_E_hsiebk4f-fntk-r8s8-lrxo-69c42b5906ac1][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

*Extent: E+W*

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

## 6. Prescribed substances: release into the air, water or land[^M_E_0d3b2h0v-edxa-42ip-po8y-69c42b5906a44][^V_S_d50db7c0-8209-f592-5405-ad5b5ac24cfb][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

*Extent: S*

(1) The description of substances set out in Schedule 4 are prescribed pursuant to section 2(5) of the Act as substances the release of which into the air is subject to control under sections 6 and 7 of the Act.

(2) Subject to paragraphs (4) and (5),[^c-f5844a70a1adc4924d09a55099fa655d] the descriptions of substances set out in column 1 of Schedule 5[^V_S_747a0c46-2b7b-13e5-e30a-8ec92367a0a0] are so prescribed as substances the release of which into water is subject to control under those sections.

(3) Subject to paragraphs (4) and (5), the descriptions of substances set out in Schedule 6 are so prescribed as substances the release of which into land is subject to control under those sections.

(4)[^c-15bf4f9d877a1d006d71423d058ee617] Paragraphs (2) and (3) of this regulation shall not apply in relation to any description of processes in an SED installation as designated in accordance with regulation 5(3) of these regulations.

(5) In relation to any description of processes in an SED installation as designated in accordance with regulation 5(3) of these regulations, volatile organic compounds as well as solvents contained in any products are prescribed as substances the release of which into the air, water or land as fugitive emissions is subject to control under those sections.

(6) For the purpose of this regulation, “volatile organic compounds”, “organic compounds” and “fugitive emissions” shall have the same meaning as they have for the purposes of Council Directive 1999/13/EC on the limitation of emissions of volatile organic compounds due to the use of solvents in certain activities and installations.

## SCHEDULE 1 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note) — DESCRIPTIONS OF PROCESSES (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

Regulation 3(1) *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

### CHAPTER 1: (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note) Fuel production processes, combustion processes (including power generation) (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note) AND ASSOCIATED PROCESSES (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29][^c-7bbe6f54aed26de2a7cf8f37d7696525][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

#### Section 1.1 Gasification and associated processes (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

##### PART A (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- ((a)) Reforming natural gas.[^c-bd925c690c076e89172fdb9dd6bd0ab1] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- ((aa)) Refining natural gas if that process is related to another Part A process or is likely to involve the use in any 12 month period of 1000 tonnes or more of natural gas. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (b) Odorising natural gas or liquified petroleum gas *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] if that process is related to another Part A process[^c-f5456b517ebcb0d88d87f72e9425bc5a] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (c) Producing gas from coal, lignite, oil or other carbonaccous material or from mixtures thereof other than from sewage or the biological degradation of waste *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] , unless carried on as part of a process which is a combustion process (whether or not that process falls within Section 1.3 of this Schedule)[^c-6b37888389b83fe48ad63ee704fbf910] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (d) Purifying or refining any product of any of the processes described in paragraphs (a), (b) or (c) or converting it into a different product. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

In this Section, “carbonaceous material” includes such materials as charcoal, coke, peat and rubber. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

##### PART B (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-31e28c16a4282a9d8569055111f31194][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

#### Section 3.1 Cement and lime manufacture and associated processes (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

##### PART A (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- (a) Making cement clinker. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (b) Grinding cement clinker. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (c) Any of the following processes, where the process is related to a process described in paragraph (a) or (b), namely, blending cement; putting cement into silos for bulk storage; removing cement from silos in which it has been stored in bulk; and any process involving the use of cement in bulk, including the bagging of cement and cement mixtures, the batching of ready-mixed concrete and the manufacture of concrete blocks and other cement products. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (d) The heating of calcium carbonate or calcium magnesium carbonate for the purpose of making lime *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] where the process is likely to involve the heating in any 12 month period of 5,000 tonnes or more of either substance or, in aggregate, of both.[^c-a9ec4a0081a069467315615d416b05d2] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (e) The slaking of lime for the purpose of making calcium hydroxide or calcium magnesium hydroxide where the process is related to a process described in paragraph (d) above. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

##### PART B (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- (a) Any of the following processes, if not related to a process falling within a description in Part A of this Section— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (i) storing, loading or unloading cement or cement clinker in bulk prior to further transportation in bulk; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (ii) blending cement in bulk or using cement in bulk other than at a construction site, including the bagging of cement and cement mixtures, the batching of ready-mixed concrete and the manufacture of concrete blocks and other cement products. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (b) The slaking of lime for the purpose of making calcium hydroxide or calcium magnesium hydroxide unless related to *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] and carried on as part of[^c-27881cf42b4e18d4346e26b5e94af345] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] a process falling within another description in this Schedule. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- ((c)) The heating of calcium carbonate or calcium magnesium carbonate for the purpose of making lime where the process is not likely to involve the heating in any 12 month period of 5,000 tonnes or more of either substance or, in aggregate, of both.[^c-f05c5526b314737da73700d56637e1bf] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- “coal” includes lignite; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- “designated mineral or mineral product” means— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (i) clay, sand and any other naturally occurring mineral other than coal or lignite; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (ii) metallurgical slag; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (iii) boiler or furnace ash produced from the burning of coal, coke or any other coal product; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (iv) gypsum which is a by-product of any process; and *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- “exempt location” means—[^c-3e7bfdc5b2bd1b635feec7bdbbfbffc2] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (i) any premises used for the sale of petroleum coke, coal, coke, or any coal product where the throughput of such substances at those premises in any 12 month period is in aggregate likely to be less than 10,000 tonnes; or *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (ii) any premises to which petroleum coke, coal, coke, or any coal product is supplied only for use there; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- “retail sale” means sale to the final consumer.[^c-620e0359dcbaefced9c29a11c6dad80f] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

Nothing in this Section applies to any process carried on underground. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

#### Section 3.5 Glass manufacture and production (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

##### PART A (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

The manufacture of glass frit or enamel frit and its use in any process where that process is related to its manufacture *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] and the aggregate quantity of such substances manufactured in any 12 month period is likely to be 100 tonnes or more.[^c-22154bff5ce1618bfea361a7e2a9d76e] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

##### PART B (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- (a) The manufacture of glass at any location where the person concerned has the capacity to make 5,000 tonnes or more of glass in any 12 month period, and any process involving the use of glass which is carried on at any such location in conjunction with its manufacture. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (b) The manufacture of glass where the use of lead or any lead compound is involved. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (c) The making of any glass product where lead or any lead compound has been used in the manufacture of the glass except— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (i) the making of products from lead glass blanks; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (ii) the melting, or mixing with another substance, of glass manufactured elsewhere to produce articles such as ornaments or road paint; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (d) Polishing or etching glass or glass products in the course of any manufacturing process if— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (i) hydrofluoric acid is used; or *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (ii) hydrogen fluoride may be released into the air. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- ((e)) The manufacture of glass frit or enamel frit and its use in any process where that process is related to its manufacture if not falling within Part A of this Section.[^c-d3e0c6f1d317d4a12a7d97898db8514a] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

#### Section 3.6 Ceramic production (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-b53ef013ae09829bea779976fcaa938d][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

##### PART A (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

Firing heavy clay goods or refractory material in a kiln where a reducing atmosphere is used for a purpose other than coloration. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

##### PART B (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- (a (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Firing heavy clay goods or refractory material (other than heavy clay goods) in a kiln where the process does not fall within a description in Part A of this Section. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (b (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Vapour glazing earthenware or clay with salts. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

In this Section— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- “clay” includes a blend of clay with ash, sand or other materials; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- “refractory material” means material (such as fireclay, silica, magnesite, chrome-magnesite, sillimanite, sintered alumina, beryllia and boron nitride) which is able to withstand high temperatures and to function as a furnace lining or in other similar high temperature applications. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- (d) Any process for the polymerisation or co-polymerisation of any unsaturated hydrocarbons (other than the polymerisation or co-polymerisation of a pre-formulated resin or pre-formulated gel coat which contains any unsaturated hydrocarbons, or which contains any product of a process mentioned in paragraph (b) or (c) of Part A of this Section) which is likely to involve, in any 12 month period, the polymerisation or co-polymerisation of 50 tonnes or more of unsaturated hydrocarbons or of any such products or, in aggregate, of any combination of those materials and products.[^c-b556e375d9cbfe9b80c4d13a757559d6] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- ((e)) Any process, if related to and carried on as part of a process falling within another paragraph of this Part of this Section, for the polymerisation or co-polymerisation of any pre-formulated resin or pre-formulated gel coat which contains any unsaturated hydrocarbons, or which contains any product of a process mentioned in paragraph (b) or (c) of Part A of this Section, which is likely to involve, in any 12 month period, the polymerisation or co-polymerisation of 100 tonnes or more of unsaturated hydrocarbons or of any such products or, in aggregate, of any combination of those materials and products.[^c-52d2c7a4a18658849ceb73ed44aa1a7b] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

Any process, unless related to and carried on as part of a process falling within Part A of this Section, for the polymerisation or co-polymerisation of any pre-formulated resin or pre-formulated gel coat which contains any unsaturated hydrocarbons, or which contains any product of a process mentioned in paragraph (b) or (c) of Part A of this Section, which is likely to involve, in any 12 month period, the polymerisation or co-polymerisation of 100 tonnes or more of unsaturated hydrocarbons or of any such products or, in aggregate, of any combination of those materials and products. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

#### Section 4.6 Chemical Fertiliser Production (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

##### PART A (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- (a) The manufacture of chemical fertilisers. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (b) The conversion of chemical fertilisers into granules. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

In this Section, “chemical fertilisers” means any inorganic chemical to be applied to the soil to promote plant growth; and “inorganic chemical” includes urea *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] ; and “manufacture of chemical fertilisers” shall be taken to include any process for blending chemical fertilisers which is related to a process for their manufacture.[^c-a3cddfe8ce28d785af37cfefb29ef07d] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

### CHAPTER 5: WASTE DISPOSAL AND RECYCLING (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- (a) The destruction by burning in an incinerator of any waste chemicals or waste plastic arising from the manufacture of a chemical or the manufacture of a plastic. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

  - bromine *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

  - iodine *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - lead *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - mercury *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - nitrogen *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - phosphorus *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - sulphur *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - zinc. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- ((bb)) The incineration of hazardous waste in an incineration plant, other than in an exempt hazardous waste incineration plant.[^c-0913bf04ee969685f58a60230ba74b3a] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (c) The destruction by burning of any other waste, including animal remains, otherwise than by a process related to *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] and carried on as part of[^c-6ed3de8b96e9b2908c29a8f50d5e8f5a] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] a Part B process, on premises where there is plant designed to incinerate such waste at a rate of 1 tonne or more per hour. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (d) The cleaning for reuse of metal containers used for the transport or storage of a chemical by burning out their residual content. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

##### PART B (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- (a) The destruction by burning in an incinerator other than an exempt incinerator of any waste, including animal remains, except where related to a Part A process. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (b) The cremation of human remains. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

In this section— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- “exempt hazardous waste incineration plant” means:[^M_F_10caa651-0ec7-496f-9595-b5531b1ce6c8] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (i) an incineration plant for animal carcases or remains; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (ii) an incineration plant for infectious clinical waste, provided that such waste is not rendered hazardous as a result of the presence of constituents listed in *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Annex II to Directive 91/689/EEC on hazardous waste *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_6e6b2ab3-6d2d-4846-bfc7-472132ca6b45] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]Schedule 2 of the Hazardous Waste (England and Wales) Regulations 2005 *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]Schedule 2 of the Hazardous Waste (Wales) Regulations 2005 *(Extent: W)*[^M_F_29c870f3-3f15-447c-b9a9-5b72dab3cd8f] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] other than constituent C35 in that list (infectious substances); or *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (iii) a municipal waste incineration plant also burning infectious clinical waste which is not mixed with other wastes which are rendered hazardous as a result of one of the properties listed in *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Annex III to Directive 91/689/EEC *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-17685b0eef00469d5b8b44a0de8361ee] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]Schedule 3 of the Hazardous Waste (England and Wales) Regulations 2005 *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]Schedule 3 of the Hazardous Waste (Wales) Regulations 2005 *(Extent: W)*[^c-d47e70df01253324fa6fdcdf5df175f1] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] other than property H9 in that list (infectious); *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- “exempt incinerator” means any incinerator on premises where there is plant designed to *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] incinerate waste, including animal remains[^c-3d7ce3ad32c0d07d97606e521051ecc5] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] at a rate of not more than 50 kgs per hour, not being an incinerator employed to incinerate clinical waste, sewage sludge, sewage screenings or municipal waste (as defined in Article I of *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] EC *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Directive *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] [89/369/ *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*EEC *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1989/0369)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29][^f00005]); and for the purposes of this section, the weight of waste shall be determined by reference to its weight as fed into the incinerator; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- “hazardous waste” means any solid or liquid waste as defined in[^M_F_161f95ab-c14d-471a-b736-0964edb00f6c] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Article 1(4) of Directive 91/689/EEC *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_c7dc865b-d1ff-436a-c70c-f215db684e22] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]regulation 6 of the Hazardous Waste (England and Wales) Regulations 2005 *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]regulation 6 of the Hazardous Waste (Wales) Regulations 2005 *(Extent: W)*[^c-58d28d037b7c65d4665e559b97fbef14] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] but shall not include the following waste: *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (i) combustible liquid wastes, including waste oils as defined in Article 1 of Directive 75/439/EEC on the disposal of waste oils, provided that they meet the following three criteria: *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
    - (a) the mass content of polychlorinated aromatic hydrocarbons, e.g. polychlorinated biphenyls or pentachlorinated phenol, amounts to concentrations not higher than those set out in the relevant Community legislation; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
    - (b) these wastes are not rendered hazardous by virtue of containing other constituents listed *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] in Annex II to Directive 91/689/EEC *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-41b53c9a90fcff0c5a6ba3a2a987af1f] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]in Schedule 2 to the Hazardous Waste (England and Wales) Regulations 2005 *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]in Schedule 2 to the Hazardous Waste (Wales) Regulations 2005 *(Extent: W)*[^c-4afdb156d8b6b1e7c7c44fce3d0043db] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] in quantities or in concentrations which are inconsistent with the achievement of the objectives set out in Article 4 of Directive 75/442/EEC on waste; and *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
    - (c) the net calorific value amounts to at least 30 MJ per kilogramme; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (ii) any combustible liquid wastes which cannot cause, in the flue gas directly resulting from their combustion, emissions other than those from gas oil, as defined in Article 1(1) of Directive 75/716/EEC on the approximation of the laws of Member States relating to the sulphur content of certain liquid fuels or a higher concentration of emissions than those resulting from the combustion of gas oil as so defined; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (iii) hazardous waste resulting from the exploration for and the exploitation of oil and gas resources from off-shore installations and incinerated on board; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (iv) municipal waste covered by Directives 89/369/EEC on the prevention of air pollution from new municipal waste incineration plants and 89/429/EEC on the reduction of air pollution from existing municipal waste incineration plants; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (v) sewage sludges from the treatment of municipal waste waters which are not rendered hazardous by virtue of containing constituents listed *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] in Annex II to Directive 91/689/EEC *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-41b53c9a90fcff0c5a6ba3a2a987af1f] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]in Schedule 2 to the Hazardous Waste (England and Wales) Regulations 2005 *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]in Schedule 2 to the Hazardous Waste (Wales) Regulations 2005 *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] in quantities or in concentrations which are inconsistent with the achievement of the objectives set out in the Article 4 of Directive 75/442/EEC on waste; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- (b (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] for the manufacture of any powder for *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] use as a coating material[^c-9ab6cb44aeeda7f3535002a733d4f05e] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] where there is the capacity to produce 200 tonnes or more of such powder in any 12 month period. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

In this Section, “coating material” has the same meaning as in Section 6.5 *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] , and the amount of organic solvents used in a process shall be calculated as—[^c-fc914d9d68a6e2365350b80cf0b9f662] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- ((a)) the total input of organic solvents into the process, including both solvents contained in coating materials and solvents used for cleaning or other purposes; less *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- ((b)) any organic solvents (not contained in coating materials) that are removed from the process for re-use or for recovery for re-use. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

#### Section 6.7 Timber processes (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

##### PART A (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- (a) The curing or chemical treatment as part of a manufacturing process of timber or of products wholly or mainly made of wood if any substance described in Schedule 5 is used. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (b) [^c-3d4862e7f5c6e6ec12dea805d585caff]... *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

##### PART B (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

The manufacture of products wholly or mainly of wood at any works if the process involves the sawing, drilling, sanding, shaping, turning, planing, curing or chemical treatment of wood *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] (“relevant processes”)[^c-8c126d9dfa299b5481927f4a30e10523] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] and the throughput of the works in any *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] 12 month period is likely to exceed—[^c-9589f6d77483ba15bc2996a98f6f6485] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- (i) 10,000 cubic metres, in the case of works at which wood is sawed but at which wood is not subjected to any other relevant processes or is subjected only to relevant processes which are exempt processes; or *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (ii) 1,000 cubic metres in any other case. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

For the purposes of this paragraph— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- relevant processes other than sawing are “exempt processes” where, if no sawing were carried on at the works, the activities carried on there would be treated as not falling within this Part of this Section by virtue of regulation 4(2);[^c-12a90bedbdcbc33c59b129ec93bfc3f6] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- “throughput” shall be calculated by reference to the amount of wood which is subjected to any *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] of the relevant processes[^c-2485c79a47e741dd168aa8409e55ece7] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]: but where, at the same works, wood is subject to two or more *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] relevant processes *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29], no account shall be taken of the second or any subsequent process; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- “wood” includes any product consisting wholly or mainly of wood;[^c-d3c79840fb10868877aa6b848d37613e] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] and *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- “works” includes a sawmill or any other premises on which relevant processes are carried out on wood. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

#### Section 6.8 Processes involving rubber (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

##### PART A (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note) — Nil (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

##### PART B (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- (a) The mixing, milling or blending of— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (i) natural rubber; or *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (ii) synthetic *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] organic[^c-7db0998c26ed212ef5855b7f6db11f4b] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] elastomers, *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

  if carbon black is used. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (b) Any process which converts the product of a process falling within paragraph (a) into a finished product if related to a process falling within that paragraph. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

#### Section 6.9 The treatment and processing of animal or vegetable matter (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

##### PART A (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

Any of the following processes, unless falling within a description in another Section of the Schedule or an exempt process, namely, the processing in any way whatsoever, storing or drying by the application of heal of any dead animal (or part thereof) or any *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] vegetable matter[^c-f22d5e72a339199df4cb17078d698d53] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] if the process may result in the release into water of any substance described in Schedule 5 in a quantity which, in any 12 month period, exceeds the background quantity by more than the amount specified in relation to the description of substance in column 2 of that Schedule[^c-2c3e8c61cb0c047cd739ba7115b3a27d] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]: but excluding any process for the treatment of effluent so as to permit its discharge into controlled waters or into a sewer unless the treatment process involves the drying of any material with a view to its use as an animal feedstuff. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

##### PART B (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- (a) Any process mentioned in Part A, of this Section unless an exempt process— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (i) where the process has the characteristics described in regulation 4(1)(ii) above; but[^c-e57d3b4591b19071d20f76417d0fc2a4] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (ii) may release into the air a substance described in Schedule 4 or any offensive smell noticeable outside the premises on which the process is carried on. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (b) Breeding maggots in any case where 5 kg or more of animal or of vegetable matter or, in aggregate, of both are introduced into the process in any week. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

In this Section— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- “animal” includes a bird or a fish; and *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- “exempt process” means— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (i) any process carried on on a farm or agricultural holding other than the manufacture of goods for sale; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (ii) the manufacture or preparation of food or drink for human consumption but excluding— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
    - (a) the extraction, distillation or purification of animal or vegetable oil or fat otherwise than as a process incidental to the cooking of food for human consumption; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
    - (b) any process involving the use of green offal or the boiling of blood except the cooking of food (other than tripe) for human consumption; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
    - (c) the cooking of tripe for human consumption elsewhere than on premises on which it is to be consumed; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - (iii) the fleshing, cleaning and drying of pelts of fur-bearing mammals;[^c-80fa3f6a2addf9a55f9d88b5e3f5dc9f] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - ((iv)) any process carried on in connection with the operation of a knacker’s yard, as defined in article 3(1) of the Animal By-Products Order 1992;[^c-8be882e7fa8ef279eb403aca3f0b68a1] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - ((v)) any process for the manufacture of soap not falling within a description in Part A of Section 4.2 of this Schedule; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - ((vi)) the storage of vegetable matter otherwise than as part of any prescribed process; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - ((vii)) the cleaning of shellfish shells; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - ((viii)) the manufacture of starch; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - ((ix)) the processing of animal or vegetable matter at premises for feeding a recognised pack of hounds registered under article 10 of the Animal By-Products Order 1992; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - ((x)) the salting of hides or skins, unless related to any other prescribed process; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - ((xi)) any process for composting animal or vegetable matter or a combination of both, except where that process is carried on for the purposes of cultivating mushrooms; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - ((xii)) any process for cleaning, and any related process for drying or dressing, seeds, bulbs, corms or tubers; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - ((xiii)) the drying of grain or pulses; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
  - ((xiv)) any process for the production of cotton yarn from raw cotton or for the conversion of cotton yarn into cloth; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- “food” includes drink, articles and substances of no nutritional value which are used for human consumption, and articles and substances used as ingredients in the preparation of food;[^c-eb88f989b874495605767721f5cf39da] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] and *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- “green offal” means the stomach and intestines of any animal, other than poultry or fish, and their contents. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

## SCHEDULE 2 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note) — RULES FOR THE INTERPRETATION OF SCHEDULE 1 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

Regulation 3(2) *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

**1 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-7f5722453f2cfdcae97bc326ab010914][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] These rules apply for the interpretation of Schedule 1 subject to any specific provision to the contrary in that Schedule. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

**2 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-e959e038a08c533dfde0ef2d97c1f263][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] (1 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Any description of a process includes any other process carried on at the same location by the same person as part of that process; but this rule does not apply in relation to any two or more processes described in different Sections of Schedule 1 which, accordingly, require distinct authorisation. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

(2 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] For the purposes of this paragraph, two or more processes which are described in Part A of different Sections of Chapter 4 of Schedule 1 shall be treated as if they were described in the same Section. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

**2A (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Notwithstanding the rule set out in paragraph 2, where a combustion process described in Part A of Section 1.3 of Schedule 1(a) is operated, or where one or more boilers, furnaces or other combustion appliances which are operated as part of a process so described are operated, as an inherent part of and primarily for the purpose of a process described in Part A of Section 1.1, Part A of Section 1.4 *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] ,[^c-a5fd6fefe27a3342513a4cca3a3fc9dd] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Part A of Section 2.1,[^c-2d788c97be27666deb793019181c61b2] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Part A of Section 6.3 *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] or Part A of any Section of Chapter 4 of that Schedule (“the other process”), that combustion process or, as the case may be, the operation of those boilers, furnaces or appliances shall be treated as part of the other process and not as, or as part of, a separate combustion process. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

**2B (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-dcae599ead3c4f0ba8589b61e4587fe2][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Notwithstanding the rule set out in paragraph 2, where a process of reforming natural gas described in paragraph (a) of Part A of Section 1.1 of Schedule 1 is carried on as an inherent part of and primarily for the purpose of producing a feedstock for a process described in Part A of any Section of Chapter 4 of that Schedule (“the other process”), that reforming process shall be treated as part of the other process and not as a separate process. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

**2C (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Nothwithstanding the rule set out in paragraph 2, where the same person carries on at the same location two or more Part B processes described in the provisions of Schedule 1 mentioned in any one of the following sub-paragraphs, those processes shall be treated as requiring authorisation as a single process falling within Part B of the Section first mentioned in the relevant sub-paragraph— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- (a (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Section 2.1 and Section 2.2; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (b (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Section 3.1 and Section 3.4; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (c (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Section 3.6 and Section 3.4; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (d (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Section 6.5 and Section 6.6; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (e (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Section 6.7 and paragraph (e) of Part B of Section 1.3 insofar as it relates to any process for the burning of waste wood. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

**3 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Where a person carries on a process which includes two or more processes described in the same Section of Schedule 1 those processes shall be treated as requiring authorisation as a single process; and if the processes involved are described in both Part A and Part B of the same Section, they shall all be regarded as part of a Part A process and so subject to central control. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

**3A (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Where a person carries on a process which includes two or more processes described in Part A of different Sections of Chapter 4 of Schedule 1, those processes shall be treated as a single process falling within a description determined in accordance with the rule set out in paragraph 4. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

**3B (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] (1 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Where paragraph 3A does not apply, but— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- (a (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] two or more processes falling within descriptions in Part A of any Sections of Chapter 4 of Schedule 1 are carried on at the same location by the same person; and *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (b (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] the carrying on of both or all of those processes at that location by that person is not likely to produce more than 250 tonnes of relevant products in any 12 month period, *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

those processes shall be treated as a single process falling within the description in whichever relevant Section is first mentioned in the sequence set out in paragraph 4. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

(2 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] In sub-paragraph (1), “relevant products” means any products of the processes in question, other than— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- (a (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] solid, liquid or gaseous waste; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (b (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] by-products, if the total value of all such by-products is insignificant in comparison to the total value of the output of the processes; or *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (c (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] any substance or material retained in or added to the final product formulation, not as an active ingredient, but as a diluent, stabiliser or preservative or for a similar purpose. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

**4 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-c1be1a49f6dccb30fa2352ae337174bc][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Where a process falls within two or more descriptions in Schedule 1, that process shall be regarded as falling only within that description which fits it most aptly: *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] but where two or more descriptions are equally apt and a process[^c-99b3ecd255635c40058fedcc03470dcb] *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] falls within descriptions in different Sections of Chapter 4, it shall be taken to fall within the description in whichever relevant Section is first mentioned in the sequence, 4.5; 4.2; 4. 1; 4.4; 4.3; 4.6; 4.7; 4.8; 4.9. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

**4A (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-d8fa50eaa3b21b2c615b746769188c5d][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Notwithstanding the rule set out in paragraph 4, where a process falls within the description in paragraph (bb) of Part A of Section 5.1 of Schedule 1 and Part B of any other Section in that Schedule, that process shall be regarded as falling only within the description in paragraph (bb) of Part A of Section 5.1. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

**5 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-d3289836cfce20d51cd6d91d09c29173][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Notwithstanding the rules set out in paragraphs 2 and 3— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- (a (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] the processes described in Part B of section 1.3 do not include the incidental storage, handling or shredding of tyres which are to be burned; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (b (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] the process described in paragraph (b) of Part B of Section 2.2 does not include the incidental storage or handling of scrap which is to be heated other than its loading into a furnace; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (c (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] the process described in paragraph (a) of Part B of Section 5.1 does not involve the incidental storage or handling of wastes and residues other than animal remains intended for burning in an incinerator used wholly or mainly for the incineration of such remains or. residues from the burning of such remains in such an incinerator; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (d (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] the process described in Part B of Section 6.5 does not include the cleaning of used storage drums prior to painting and their incidental handling in connection with such cleaning; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (e (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-fd8dc583025775b47184bc7bb3cf4933][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] any description of a Part B process includes any related process which would fall within paragraph (c) of Part A of Section 1.3 if it were not so related. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

**6 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-cbf2a4771ddd69b2660d5b64481bd007][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] The following activities, that is to say— *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

- (a (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] the unloading, screening, grading, mixing or otherwise handling of petroleum coke, coal, lignite, coke or any other coal product; *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
- (b (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] the unloading of iron ore or burnt pyrites, *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

for use in a prescribed process by a person other than the person carrying on the process at the place where the process is carried on shall be treated as part of that process. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

**7 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-e8865ed34e9553b2e9223fd495dbeda3][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] (1 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Where by reason of the use at different times of different fuels or different materials or the disposal at different times of different wastes, processes of different descriptions are carried out with the same plant or machinery and those processes include one or more Part A processes and one or more other processes, the other processes shall be regarded as within the descriptions of the Part A processes. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

(2 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Where by reason of such use or disposal as is mentioned in paragraph (1), processes of different descriptions are carried out with the same plant or machinery and those processes include one or more Part B processes and one or more other processes (but no Part A processes), all those processes shall be regarded as within the descriptions of the Part B processes. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

(3 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-fa4e8a6aeacc556d71b3b53e84cc5154][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Where by reason of such use or disposal as is mentioned in sub-paragraph (1), processes of different descriptions are carried out with the same plant and machinery and those processes include Part B processes falling within different Sections of Schedule 1 (but no Part A processes), those processes shall, notwithstanding the rule set out in paragraph 2, be treated as a single Part B process falling within the description in whichever of those Sections first appears in that Schedule. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

**7A (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-ae83e180eda8293fe099e389eabe3f36][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] The reference to “any other process” in paragraph 2 and the references to “other processes” in paragraph 7 do not include references to a process (other than one described in Schedule 1) of loading or unloading any ship or other vessel. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

**8 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-7eeb14eadb7910ada1db737361b6adab][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] Where in the course of, or as a process ancillary to, any prescribed process the person carrying on that process uses, treats or disposes of waste at the same location (whether as fuel or otherwise), the use, treatment or disposal of that waste shall, notwithstanding the rule set out in paragraph 2, be regarded as falling within the description of that process, whether the waste was produced by the person carrying on the process or acquired by him for such use, treatment or disposal. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

**8A (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-da1d4798b97cbdcbd8f29ece327038ea][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] The rule in paragraph 8 shall not apply in the case of the incineration of hazardous waste in an incineration plant, as described in paragraph (bb) of Part A of Section 5.1 of Schedule 1, when the prescribed process is a Part B process. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

**9 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-752325c0c662c07fe89ec68cb9f9fd27][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] References in Schedule 1 and this Schedule to related processes are references to separate processes carried on by the same person at the same location. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

**10 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-b52ef831a06fcd4c1d9b0c4fbcc896be][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

**11 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-05c9d6ba479ef74977c8488658c7ed9a][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29] References to a process involving the release of a substance falling within a description in Schedule 4 or 5 hereto do not affect the application of paragraphs (1) and (2) of regulation 4. *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

## SCHEDULE 3 (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note) — DATE FROM WHICH AUTHORISATION IS REQUIRED UNDER SECTION 6 OF THE ACT (Extent: E+W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29][^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]

Regulation 3(1) and (3) *(Extent: E+W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d11170686347f51849bf8e7498497e29]
